HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000240/B007","root_001069/B001","root_001520/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_05584dba8f026975916e","text":"3. **Visible source inside the looking pedagogy.** Evidence: `ع ي ن` B001 seeing eye, B002 face-to-face seeing; `ن ظ ر` B001 looking later; `ج ر ي` B007 keeping pace/running with. Structural cue: the surah later commands looking at created signs. Role: the spring is a visible sign whose flow keeps pace with the reader’s perception. Abductive move: what is enjoyed in the garden also teaches the structure of creation. Reading change: the ayah reads as both amenity and demonstrative sign.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001069/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1057fc9e7f128a8f72fe","text":"1. **The spring is continuing provision rather than a one-time supply.** **Lexical:** `ع ي ن` B006, \"the springing water-eye,\" and `ج ر ي` B001, flowing, together with B006, \"ongoing provision or grant.\" **Cue:** the spring sits inside a sequence of sustained ease, satisfaction, shelter, quiet, couches, cups, cushions, and carpets in 88:8-16. **Role:** `عين` supplies the source, B001 supplies motion from it, and B006 gives that motion the temporal role of maintaining provision. **Abduction:** I infer that the flow services the surrounding habitat; no pipe or act of distribution is stated. **Reading change:** `جارية` means more than visually running water: it makes the garden's benefits renewable and continuous.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001069/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_247745125269f7cccc3a","text":"4. **The nearby camel clarifies that the spring answers dependence rather than displaying luxury alone.** **Lexical:** fixed `ع ي ن` B006 and `ج ر ي` B006 are sharpened by `ء ب ل` B002 in 88:17, \"camels sufficing without water\" through fresh herbage. **Cue:** the water source and the camel occur at opposite ends of the focus window. **Role:** the camel supplies a biological counter-model of water use; the spring supplies uninterrupted external availability. **Abduction:** I infer that their placement invites comparison between resilience under scarcity and abundance that removes scarcity. **Reading change:** the running spring reads as one designed solution to bodily dependence, made more visible by a creature designed to manage that dependence differently.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001069/B001","root_001069/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2c9a0e00278915ce4bc6","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:12:2\",\"root_001069\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kaynak sözcüğünün göz ve yüz yüze görmeyi de çağrıştırması, akan suyu biraz sonra başlayacak yaratılışı inceleme çağrısına açılan görünür bir bilgi kaynağına dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000240/B001","root_001069/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2d4786c2c3cb45875e4b","text":"1. **Running spring as reversal of the hot spring.** Evidence: `ع ي ن` B006 springing water-eye; `ج ر ي` B001 running/flowing; earlier `ء ن ي` B003 heat limit and `س ق ي` B001 forced drink. Structural cue: same `عين` appears in both punishment and garden, but one is `آنية` and one `جارية`. Role: flow replaces heat-completion. Abductive move: water’s moral quality is shown by its motion and context. Reading change: the ayah reads as the restored function of water.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000240/B006","root_001069/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_67d5a0a30dae34beb08c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:12:2\",\"root_001069\",[\"B006\"]],[\"88:12:3\",\"root_000240\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yakındaki deve, kesintisiz dış su kaynağı ile su kıtlığına dayanıklı beden arasında iki ayrı yaşamı sürdürme çözümünü görünür kılar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000240/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9416a651c5690e8a6948","text":"3. **Flow is a reliable course that ordered objects are arranged around.** **Lexical:** `ج ر ي` B002, \"habitual course or way.\" **Cue:** fixed flow in 88:12 is followed by raised, placed, rowed, and spread furnishings in 88:13-16, each a participial state of deliberate spatial order. **Role:** the course branch supplies temporal regularity; the furnishings translate that regularity into stable spatial access. **Abduction:** I infer design coordination between water and objects from their sequence. **Reading change:** the ayah reads as the moving axis of an otherwise settled interior, regular circulation embedded in stable arrangement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001069/B001","root_001069/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9ee928d855dbe6d1f68d","text":"2. **The source-eye prepares the transition from enjoyment to inspection.** **Lexical:** `ع ي ن` B001, the seeing eye, and B002, direct seeing, coactivated with the spring branch B006. **Cue:** five ayat after the spring, 88:17 explicitly asks the audience to look (`يَنظُرُونَ`) at the camel and how it was formed. **Role:** the spring is a material source within the habitat, while the dormant seeing branches make \"source\" itself an object and instrument of attention. **Abduction:** I infer that the lexical eye primes the later command to inspect provisioning design. **Reading change:** the spring becomes an epistemic aperture: its visible flow invites inquiry into how life-supporting systems are made.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000240/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a1cc771a785baed15d87","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:12:3\",\"root_000240\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Akışın kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b4037617677a84637f2b","text":"88:13-16 confirmed the ongoing-provision reading by arranging stable uses around the flow.
